- **evidence-argument-chain**: 用于将案件中的各项证据与法律主张系统性地对应挂钩、构建完整证据链时触发。 典型触发场景： - 构建"证据-主张"对应关系，把每项证据归位到具体的法律主张与构成要件之下； - 庭审前编制证据目录并逐项写明证明目的； - 对方提出反驳或异议后，需要重新组织、重组证据链以回应； - 案件存在多个争议焦点，需按焦点将证据分组归类； - 评估现有证据是否足以支撑全部主张，识别证据链薄弱环节与缺口并给出补强建议。 核心是建立"主张 → 要件 → 证据 → 证明力评估"的完整映射，确保每个主张都有充分证据、每项证据都有明确证明目的。 不适用于：单纯的证据真伪/三性鉴别（仅审单份证据可信度时无须建链）、法律检索或法条释义、 文书全文起草排版、事实时间线梳理（无主张-要件映射需求时）、以及非中国大陆法语境的案件。 ([FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon](https://github.com/FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon/tree/HEAD/hongzhi-core/skills/evidence-argument-chain) · ★2)

- **evo-react-component**: Generates the React 19 structural layer for a new evo-web component from an approved manifest.json. Produces index.tsx (React 19 component), and __tests__/index.spec.tsx (Vitest test scaffold). Called inline by /evo-component as Step 5. Use this skill whenever the user says "generate the React layer", "create the React files from the manifest", "generate the evo-react component", or when /evo-component delegates Step 5 to this skill. Do NOT confuse with /evo-migrate-react which ports existing ebayui-core-react components — this skill generates new components from scratch. ([eBay/evo-web](https://github.com/eBay/evo-web/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/evo-react-component) · ★51)
